# Minecraft 26.2 — GUI / text-rendering / client-entry reference (javap-backed)

Reverse-engineered **only** from the artifacts on this machine. Every claim is preceded by the exact
command that produced it and its raw output. Nothing under `/tmp/mcapi/full` was modified.

### 0.1 Defect A — `maps.py:parse_yarn` never parses methods

tiny-v2 method lines are `\tm\t<desc>\t<inter>\t<named>` (5 tab-fields after `split('\t')`), but the
parser requires `len(parts) >= 6` and reads `parts[5]`. Therefore
`maps.yarn()[cls]['methods']` is **always empty** (the helper's `yarn()` output for methods cannot be
trusted). Field parsing (`len >= 5`) happens to be correct.

```text
$ head -5 /tmp/mc12111/yarn/mappings/mappings.tiny
tiny	2	0	intermediary	named
c	net/minecraft/class_3720	net/minecraft/block/entity/BlastFurnaceBlockEntity
	f	Lnet/minecraft/class_2561;	field_63037	CONTAINER_NAME_TEXT
	m	(Lnet/minecraft/class_2338;Lnet/minecraft/class_2680;)V	<init>	<init>
		p	1		pos

$ awk 'NR>1 && /\tm\t/ {print; c++} c>3{exit}' /tmp/mc12111/yarn/mappings/mappings.tiny | cat -A | head -3
^Im^I(Lnet/minecraft/class_2338;Lnet/minecraft/class_2680;)V^I<init>^I<init>$
^Im^I(Lnet/minecraft/class_1937;Lnet/minecraft/class_2338;Lnet/minecraft/class_2680;Lnet/minecraft/class_3721;)V^Imethod_31659^IserverTick$
^Im^I(Lnet/minecraft/class_1937;Lnet/minecraft/class_2338;Ljava/util/List;)V^Imethod_20218^IapplyParticlesToRaiders$
```

### 0.2 Defect B — `maps.py:parse_official` files line-number-less methods as *fields*

Its regex requires a `^(\d+):(\d+):` prefix; any method line without one falls through to the field
branch. `net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker` therefore reports **zero** methods and three bogus fields:

```text
$ sed -n "$(grep -n '^net\.minecraft\.client\.DeltaTracker -> ' /tmp/mc12111/client.txt | head -1 | cut -d: -f1),+6p" /tmp/mc12111/client.txt
net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker -> gez:
# {"fileName":"DeltaTracker.java","id":"sourceFile"}
    net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker ZERO -> a
    net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker ONE -> b
    float getGameTimeDeltaTicks() -> a
    float getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(boolean) -> a
    float getRealtimeDeltaTicks() -> b
```

**Consequence for this report:** every 1.21.11 *official* member list below was produced by dumping the
**raw ProGuard block** from `client.txt` (helper `dumpoff.py`), never from `maps.official()`.
Class-level yarn→official mapping still uses `maps.yarn2official()` (unaffected).

---

```text
$ find /tmp/mcapi/full -iname '*MinecraftClient*'
(no output — NOT FOUND)
```

## 1.4 `net.fabricmc.api.ClientModInitializer` — **NOT FOUND in vanilla** (verified 4 ways)

```text
$ unzip -l ~/.gradle/caches/fabric-loom/minecraftMaven/net/minecraft/minecraft-merged-deobf/26.2/minecraft-merged-deobf-26.2.jar | grep -ci "fabricmc"
0

$ find /tmp/mcapi/full -iname '*fabric*' | wc -l
0

$ grep -rl "FabricLoader" /tmp/mcapi/full | wc -l
0

$ unzip -l ~/.gradle/caches/fabric-loom/minecraftMaven/net/minecraft/minecraft-merged-deobf/26.2/minecraft-merged-deobf-26.2.jar | grep -c "net/fabricmc"
0

$ find /tmp/mcapi/full -iname '*ClientModInitializer*'
(no output — NOT FOUND)

$ grep -rl "ClientModInitializer" /tmp/mcapi/full
(no output — NOT FOUND)
```

**Vanilla-equivalent entry hooks that DO exist in 26.2** (all verified by the `javap` dumps above):

| hook | exact signature | evidence |
|---|---|---|
| process entry | `net.minecraft.client.main.Main#main(java.lang.String[])` | `javap -p net.minecraft.client.main.Main` |
| client construction | `public net.minecraft.client.Minecraft(net.minecraft.client.main.GameConfig)` | `javap -p net.minecraft.client.Minecraft` |
| client main loop | `public void run()` | same |
| per-frame | `public void renderFrame(boolean)` | same |
| per-tick | `public void tick()` | same |
| shutdown | `public void stop()`, `public void close()` | same |
| global accessor | `public static net.minecraft.client.Minecraft getInstance()` | same |

Changes: the 1.21.11 ctor `Window(WindowEventHandler, ScreenManager, DisplayData, String, String)`
became `Window(WindowEventHandler, DisplayData, String, boolean, String, MonitorManager, GpuBackend)`
(a `ScreenManager`/`MonitorManager` refactor + explicit `GpuBackend`); `updateDisplay`/`updateFullscreen`/
`updateVsync` were replaced by `updateFullscreenIfChanged()`, `isSoftScreen()`, `backend()`,
`getAppropriateLineWidth()` is unchanged. GUI-scale accessors `getGuiScaledWidth()/getGuiScaledHeight()/
getGuiScale()/calculateScale(int,boolean)` are unchanged.

**Net:** in 1.21.11 the GUI render-state records lived in `net.minecraft.client.gui.render.state[.pip]`.
In 26.2 they live in `net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.gui[.pip]`. `net.minecraft.client.gui.render`
survives but now holds only the renderer + atlas + `TextureSetup` + `pip` *renderers*.

The classes named in the task that **do not** exist anywhere in 26.2:

## 2.4 `GuiGraphics` → **`GuiGraphicsExtractor`** (the single most important port change)

```text
$ find /tmp/mcapi/full -name 'GuiGraphics*'
/tmp/mcapi/full/net/minecraft/client/gui/GuiGraphicsExtractor$HoveredTextEffects.class
/tmp/mcapi/full/net/minecraft/client/gui/GuiGraphicsExtractor$ScissorStack.class
/tmp/mcapi/full/net/minecraft/client/gui/GuiGraphicsExtractor.class
/tmp/mcapi/full/net/minecraft/client/gui/GuiGraphicsExtractor$RenderingTextCollector.class

$ find /tmp/mcapi/full -iname '*DrawContext*'
(no output — NOT FOUND)

$ unzip -l ~/.gradle/caches/fabric-loom/minecraftMaven/net/minecraft/minecraft-merged-deobf/26.2/minecraft-merged-deobf-26.2.jar | grep -i "GuiGraphics"
     2047  1980-02-01 00:00   net/minecraft/client/gui/GuiGraphicsExtractor$HoveredTextEffects.class
     2310  1980-02-01 00:00   net/minecraft/client/gui/GuiGraphicsExtractor$ScissorStack.class
    56254  1980-02-01 00:00   net/minecraft/client/gui/GuiGraphicsExtractor.class

```

`net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics` (1.21.11 official; yarn `net.minecraft.client.gui.DrawContext`,
obf `gir`) **DOES NOT EXIST in 26.2**. Its replacement is
**`net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor`**.

### 2.4.1 FULL `javap -p` of `GuiGraphicsExtractor`

⇒ every `draw*` / `render*` / `submit*` GUI method name is gone; the replacements are
`text` / `centeredText` / `item` / `fakeItem` / `itemDecorations` / `tooltip` / `outline` /
`map` / `entity` / `skin` / `book` / `bannerPattern` / `profilerChart` / `extractDeferredElements`,
and the `submit*` family collapsed into the `blit` / `fill` / `innerFill` sinks. There is **no
`submitSignRenderState` equivalent** (`grep -ci sign GuiGraphicsExtractor.txt` → `0`).

## 2.6 `DrawContext#drawTexture` — precise answer

**1.21.11 status.** `drawTexture` is a **yarn** name only. The yarn member list (raw tiny parse, because
of Defect A) is:

```text
$ python3 - <<'EOF'
YARN="/tmp/mc12111/yarn/mappings/mappings.tiny"
want='net.minecraft.client.gui.DrawContext'; cur=None; res=[]
for line in open(YARN, encoding='utf-8'):
    p=line.rstrip('\n').split('\t')
    if p[0]=='c': cur=p[2].replace('/','.'); continue
    if cur!=want or len(p)<5 or p[0]!='': continue
    if p[1]=='m': res.append((p[4],p[2]))
for n,d in sorted(res):
    if 'exture' in n or 'Blit' in n or 'blit' in n or 'TexturedQuad' in n: print(f'  {n}{d}')
EOF
```

**Answer to "does `DrawContext#drawTexture` still exist in 26.2 under `GuiGraphics`?"**
`net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics` does not exist in 26.2, and the yarn method name `drawTexture`
never existed as an official name. **NOT FOUND.** The replacement is
`GuiGraphicsExtractor#blit(...)` (same 4 overload shapes as 1.21.11), with
`GuiGraphicsExtractor#blitSprite(...)` for sprite/GUI-texture blits. `renderTexture`/`drawTexture`
helpers are not present:

```text
$ javap -p -classpath /tmp/mcapi/full net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor | grep -c 'drawTexture\|renderTexture\|drawTexturedQuad'
0
```

1.21.11 official signatures are from the raw `client.txt` block in §2.6; 26.2 signatures from §2.4.1.

### 2.8.12 The complete chain, in one place

```text
Minecraft.run()
 └─ Minecraft.renderFrame(boolean)                              [§1.2]
     └─ GameRenderer.render(DeltaTracker, boolean)              [§2.8.7 bytecode, offset 374-396]
         ├─ profiler.push("gui")
         ├─ GuiRenderer.render()                                [§2.8.10]
         │    ├─ prepare()                                      [§2.8.9]
         │    │   ├─ preparePictureInPicture()
         │    │   ├─ prepareItemElements()                      → GuiItemAtlas.getOrUpdate(...)
         │    │   ├─ prepareText()                              → GuiRenderState.forEachText(lambda$prepareText$0)
         │    │   │    └─ GuiTextRenderState.ensurePrepared()   → Font$PreparedText
         │    │   │         └─ PreparedText.visit(Font$GlyphVisitor)  [GuiRenderer$1.acceptRenderable]
         │    │   │              └─ new GlyphRenderState(pose, TextRenderable, scissor)
         │    │   │                 └─ GuiRenderState.addGlyphToCurrentLayer(...)   [§2.8.11]
         │    │   ├─ GuiRenderState.sortElements(ELEMENT_SORT_COMPARATOR)
         │    │   ├─ addElementsToMeshes(TraverseRange.BEFORE_BLUR)
         │    │   │    └─ addElementToMesh(GuiElementRenderState)   [§2.8.9]
         │    │   │         ├─ pipeline = e.pipeline(); tex = e.textureSetup(); scissor = e.scissorArea()
         │    │   │         ├─ StagedVertexBuffer.appendDraw(pipeline.getVertexFormatBinding(0), pipeline.getPrimitiveTopology())
         │    │   │         │    └─ draws.add(new GuiRenderer$Draw(draw, pipeline, textureSetup, scissorArea))
         │    │   │         └─ e.buildVertices(vertexBuffer.getVertexBuilder(draw))    <-- vertices written here
         │    │   └─ addElementsToMeshes(TraverseRange.AFTER_BLUR)
         │    ├─ StagedVertexBuffer.upload()
         │    ├─ draw()                                         [§2.8.10 executeDraw]
         │    │    └─ per Draw: RenderPass.setPipeline / setVertexBuffer / enableScissor|disableScissor
         │    │                 / bindTexture("Sampler0"|"Sampler1"|"Sampler2")
         │    │                 / setIndexBuffer / drawIndexed(...)   <-- GPU submission
         │    ├─ StagedVertexBuffer.endDraw(); endFrame()
         │    ├─ draws.clear()
         │    └─ GuiRenderState.reset()
         └─ GuiRenderer.endFrame()                              [§2.8.10]

The recording half (BEFORE GameRenderer.render):
Gui.extractRenderState(DeltaTracker, boolean, boolean)          [§2.8.6]
 ├─ GuiRenderState.reset()
 ├─ new GuiGraphicsExtractor(minecraft, guiRenderState, mouseX, mouseY)
 ├─ Hud.extractRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor, DeltaTracker)
 ├─ Overlay.extractRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor, int, int, float)
 ├─ Screen.extractRenderStateWithTooltipAndSubtitles(GuiGraphicsExtractor, int, int, float)
 └─ GuiGraphicsExtractor.applyCursor(Window)

Every GuiGraphicsExtractor draw call:
  fill / fillGradient / outline  -> new ColoredRectangleRenderState(...)  -> GuiRenderState.addGuiElement  [§2.8.1]
  blit / blitSprite              -> new BlitRenderState / TiledBlitRenderState -> GuiRenderState.addGuiElement [§2.8.2]
  text / centeredText            -> new GuiTextRenderState(...)           -> GuiRenderState.addText        [§2.8.3]
  item / fakeItem                -> new GuiItemRenderState(...)           -> GuiRenderState.addItem
  entity/skin/book/banner/profilerChart -> ...pip.PictureInPictureRenderState -> GuiRenderState.addPicturesInPictureState
```

`GuiEventListener` keeps its name but **all input events now take packet-style event objects**
(`net.minecraft.client.input.MouseButtonEvent`, `KeyEvent`, `CharacterEvent`, `PreeditEvent`) — this is
already true in 1.21.11 for mouse/key; 26.2 **adds** `preeditUpdated(PreeditEvent)` and
`shouldTakeFocusAfterInteraction()` was already present.

Renames: `render(GuiGraphics,int,int,float)` → `extractRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor,int,int,float)`
(now `final`); `renderWidget(...)` → `abstract void extractWidgetRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor,int,int,float)`;
`renderScrollingStringOverContents(ActiveTextCollector,Component,int)` → `extractScrollingStringOverContents(...)`;
`renderTooltip`/tooltip handling → `extractTooltipForNextRenderPass(GuiGraphicsExtractor,int,int)` +
`handleCursor(GuiGraphicsExtractor)` (was `handleCursor(GuiGraphics)`); fields `x`,`y` became `private`
(accessors `getX()/setX()/getY()/setY()` unchanged). `active`, `visible`, `alpha`, `isHovered` fields
retained.

`Screen#render(GuiGraphics,int,int,float)`. It has

```java
public void extractRenderState(net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor, int, int, float);
public final void extractRenderStateWithTooltipAndSubtitles(net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor, int, int, float);
public void extractBackground(net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor, int, int, float);
```

Rename map for `Screen`:

| 1.21.11 official | 26.2 | status |
|---|---|---|
| `void render(GuiGraphics,int,int,float)` | `void extractRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor,int,int,float)` | **renamed** |
| `void renderWithTooltipAndSubtitles(GuiGraphics,int,int,float)` | `final void extractRenderStateWithTooltipAndSubtitles(GuiGraphicsExtractor,int,int,float)` | **renamed, now final** |
| `void renderBackground(GuiGraphics,int,int,float)` | `void extractBackground(GuiGraphicsExtractor,int,int,float)` | **renamed** |
| `void renderBlurredBackground(GuiGraphics)` | `protected void extractBlurredBackground(GuiGraphicsExtractor)` | renamed |
| `void renderPanorama(GuiGraphics,float)` | `protected void extractPanorama(GuiGraphicsExtractor,float)` | renamed |
| `void renderMenuBackground(GuiGraphics)` / `(GuiGraphics,int,int,int,int)` | `protected void extractMenuBackground(GuiGraphicsExtractor)` / `(GuiGraphicsExtractor,int,int,int,int)` | renamed |
| `void renderMenuBackgroundTexture(GuiGraphics,Identifier,int,int,float,float,int,int)` | `static void extractMenuBackgroundTexture(GuiGraphicsExtractor,Identifier,int,int,float,float,int,int)` | renamed |
| `void renderTransparentBackground(GuiGraphics)` | `void extractTransparentBackground(GuiGraphicsExtractor)` | renamed |
| `boolean panoramaShouldSpin()` | **NOT FOUND** | **REMOVED** (`grep -c panoramaShouldSpin` → `0`) |
| `void init(int,int)` / `void init()` | `final void init(int,int)` / `protected void init()` | unchanged signatures; `init(int,int)` now `final` |
| `boolean keyPressed(KeyEvent)` | unchanged | unchanged |
| `T addRenderableWidget(T)` / `addRenderableOnly` / `addWidget` | unchanged | unchanged |
| `void mouseClicked(...)` | inherited from `AbstractContainerEventHandler`/`GuiEventListener` (`MouseButtonEvent`) | unchanged |

`panoramaShouldSpin` removal evidence:

```text
$ javap -p -classpath /tmp/mcapi/full net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen | grep -c panoramaShouldSpin
0
```

Renames: `render`→`extractRenderState`, `renderContents`→`extractContents`, `renderCarriedItem`→`extractCarriedItem`,
`renderSlots`→`extractSlots`, `renderSlot`→`extractSlot`, `renderLabels`→`extractLabels`,
`renderTooltip`→`extractTooltip`, `renderFloatingItem`→`extractFloatingItem`,
`renderSlotHighlightBack/Front`→`extractSlotHighlightBack/Front`. **`renderSnapbackItem` and
`clearDraggingState` present in 1.21.11 are GONE in 26.2**; `init()` is `protected void init()` (no args),
`mouseClicked(MouseButtonEvent,boolean)`, `keyPressed(KeyEvent)`, `slotClicked(Slot,int,int,ContainerInput)`
(note: `ClickType` → `ContainerInput`).

⇒ `navigation` is **unchanged structurally** (`FocusNavigationEvent` + `ArrowNavigation`/`InitialFocus`/
`TabNavigation`, `ScreenAxis`, `ScreenDirection`, `ScreenPosition`, `ScreenRectangle`), and
`ScreenRectangle`'s full member set (ctor, `empty`, `of`, `step`, `getLength`, `getBoundInDirection`,
`getBorder`, `overlaps`, `overlapsInAxis`, `getCenterInAxis`, `intersection`, `intersects`, `encompasses`,
`top/bottom/left/right`, `containsPoint`, `transformAxisAligned`, `transformMaxBounds`) is
**identical between 1.21.11 and 26.2**.

`components` **structural** changes relevant to porting:

- The `Renderable` contract rename (`render` → `extractRenderState`) applies to **every** widget
  (`AbstractWidget`, `ImageWidget`, `StringWidget`, `CycleButton`, `Checkbox`, `EditBox`, `MultiLineEditBox`,
  `AbstractSelectionList`, `ObjectSelectionList`, `MultiLineLabel`, `SubtitleOverlay`, `DebugScreenOverlay`, …).
- `net.minecraft.client.gui.components` gained `AbstractContainerWidget`, `ScrollableLayout`,
  `PlayerFaceExtractor`, `PlayerFaceWidget`, `ItemDisplayWidget`, `PlayerSkinWidget`, `TextCursorUtils`,
  `ResettableOptionWidget`, `SelectableEntry`, `IMEPreeditOverlay`, `LerpingBossEvent`, `WidgetSprites`,
  `WidgetTooltipHolder`, `FocusableTextWidget`, `FittingMultiLineTextWidget`, `MultiLineTextWidget`,
  `PopupScreen`, `TabButton`, `FriendsButton`.
- `ChatComponent` gained an internal graphics-access abstraction
  (`ChatComponent$ChatGraphicsAccess`, `$DrawingBackgroundGraphicsAccess`, `$DrawingFocusedGraphicsAccess`,
  `$ClickableTextOnlyGraphicsAccess`, `$AlphaCalculator`, `$DisplayMode`, `$Draft`, `$State`).
- New subpackage `net.minecraft.client.gui.contextualbar`; `DebugScreenOverlay` now delegates to
  `net.minecraft.client.gui.components.debug.*` (`DebugScreenEntryList` is a field on `Minecraft`).
- `net.minecraft.client.gui.narration` and `net.minecraft.client.gui.spectator` unchanged.

---

### 3.4.1 Is `net.minecraft.network.chat.Text` still present in 26.2? **NO — NOT FOUND**

```text
$ ls /tmp/mcapi/full/net/minecraft/network/chat/ | grep -i "^Text"
TextColor$1.class
TextColor.class

$ find /tmp/mcapi/full -name "Text.class" -o -name 'Text$*.class'
(no output — NOT FOUND)

$ javap -p -classpath /tmp/mcapi/full net.minecraft.network.chat.Text
错误: 找不到类: net.minecraft.network.chat.Text
[javap exit code 1]
```

⇒ `net.minecraft.network.chat.Text` remains **removed** (it was removed in 1.19); the only `Text*`
classes in `network.chat` are `TextColor` (+anonymous). Use
`net.minecraft.network.chat.Component.literal(...)` / `.translatable(...)` / `.empty()` /
`.nullToEmpty(...)`.

All requested `Component` statics/defaults verified present in 26.2 (from §3.4 dump):
`literal(String)`, `translatable(String)`, `translatable(String,Object...)`,
`translatableEscape`, `translatableWithFallback(String,String[,Object...])`, `empty()`,
`nullToEmpty(String)`, `keybind(String)`, `nbt(...)`, `score(...)`, `selector(...)`, `object(...)`,
`translationArg(...)` overloads; instance `getString()`, `getString(int)`, `copy()`, `plainCopy()`,
`getSiblings()`, `getStyle()`, `getContents()`, `getVisualOrderText()`, `visit(...)`, `toFlatList(...)`,
`contains(Component)`, `tryCollapseToString()`.
`append` / `withStyle` / `setStyle` / `withColor` live on `MutableComponent` (see §3.4 dump), not on
`Component`.

## 3.5 `renderText` / `drawText` helpers in `GuiGraphicsExtractor`

There is **no** `renderText`/`drawText` helper in 26.2 — those names belong to the removed API surface:

```text
$ javap -p -classpath /tmp/mcapi/full net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor | grep -c 'renderText\|drawText'
0
```

Replacements: `GuiGraphicsExtractor#text(...)` (6 overloads), `#centeredText(...)` (3),
`#textWithWordWrap(...)` (2), `#textWithBackdrop(...)`, plus the `ActiveTextCollector` returned by
`#textRenderer(...)` (for hit-testable/clickable text, scrolling text, and narration-aware text) — see
§2.4.1 and §3.3.

---

| `net.minecraft.client.font.TextRenderer#trimToWidth` | `plainSubstrByWidth` / `substrByWidth` | same | renamed before 1.21.11 | §3.1.1 |
| `net.minecraft.client.font.TextRenderer#getWrappedLinesHeight` | `wordWrapHeight(FormattedText,int)` | same | renamed before 1.21.11 | §3.1.1 |
| `net.minecraft.client.font.TextRenderer#draw/drawWithShadow` | *(no such official member)* | **NOT FOUND** | removed → `prepareText(...)` + `ActiveTextCollector` + `GuiGraphicsExtractor#text` | §3.1.1 |
| `net.minecraft.client.font.StringRenderOutput` | — | **NOT FOUND** → `net.minecraft.client.gui.font.TextRenderable` (+`$Styled`), `Font$PreparedTextBuilder` | **replaced** | §3.3 `javap -p` |
| `net.minecraft.util.OrderedText` | `net.minecraft.util.FormattedCharSequence` | `net.minecraft.util.FormattedCharSequence` | renamed before 1.21.11; unchanged | §3.3 |
| `net.minecraft.text.Style` | `net.minecraft.network.chat.Style` | `net.minecraft.network.chat.Style` | unchanged; +shadow-color API | §3.3 |
| `net.minecraft.text.Text` | `net.minecraft.network.chat.Component` | `net.minecraft.network.chat.Component` | unchanged | §3.4 |
| `net.minecraft.text.MutableText` | `net.minecraft.network.chat.MutableComponent` | `net.minecraft.network.chat.MutableComponent` | unchanged | §3.4 |
| — | `net.minecraft.network.chat.Text` | **NOT FOUND** | **removed** (since 1.19) | §3.4.1 `ls \| grep` + `find` + failing `javap` (exit 1) |
| `net.minecraft.client.gui.DrawContext#renderText`(helper) | *(no such member)* | **NOT FOUND** → `GuiGraphicsExtractor#text(...)` | removed | §3.5 `grep -c` → `0` |
